id=\"S4.T3.14.12.1.1.m1.1c\">\\uparrow</annotation><annotation encoding=\"application/x-llamapun\" id=\"S4.T3.14.12.1.1.m1.1d\">↑</annotation></semantics></math></span></td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table>\n</figure><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n<div class=\"ltx_para ltx_noindent\" id=\"S4.SS3.SSS2.p3\">\n<p class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S4.SS3.SSS2.p3.1\">These results demonstrate Zep’s ability to enhance performance across model scales, with the most pronounced improvements observed in complex and nuanced question types when paired with more capable models. The latency improvements are particularly noteworthy, with Zep reducing response times by approximately 90% while maintaining higher accuracy.</p><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n</div>\n<div class=\"ltx_para ltx_noindent\" id=\"S4.SS3.SSS2.p4\">\n<p class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S4.SS3.SSS2.p4.1\">The decrease in performance for single-session-assistant questions—17.7% for gpt-4o and 9.06% for gpt-4o-mini—represents a notable exception to Zep’s otherwise consistent improvements, and suggest further research and engineering work is needed.</p><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n</div>\n</section>\n</section>\n</section>\n<section class=\"ltx_section\" id=\"S5\">\n<h2 class=\"ltx_title ltx_title_section\">\n<span class=\"ltx_tag ltx_tag_section\">5 </span>Conclusion</h2><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n<div class=\"ltx_para ltx_noindent\" id=\"S5.p1\">\n<p class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S5.p1.1\">We have introduced Zep, a graph-based approach to LLM memory that incorporates semantic and episodic memory alongside entity and community summaries. Our evaluations demonstrate that Zep achieves state-of-the-art performance on existing memory benchmarks while reducing token costs and operating at significantly lower latencies.</p><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n</div>\n<div class=\"ltx_para ltx_noindent\" id=\"S5.p2\">\n<p class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S5.p2.1\">The results achieved with Graphiti and Zep, while impressive, likely represent only initial advances in graph-based memory systems. Multiple research avenues could build upon these frameworks, including integration of other GraphRAG approaches into the Zep paradigm and novel extensions of our work.</p><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n</div>\n<div class=\"ltx_para ltx_noindent\" id=\"S5.p3\">\n<p class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S5.p3.1\">Research has already demonstrated the value of fine-tuned models for LLM-based entity and edge extraction within the GraphRAG paradigm, improving accuracy while reducing costs and latency <cite class=\"ltx_cite ltx_citemacro_cite\">[<a class=\"ltx_ref\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/html/2501.13956v1#bib.bib19\" title=\"\">19</a>]</cite><cite class=\"ltx_cite ltx_citemacro_cite\">[<a class=\"ltx_ref\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/html/2501.13956v1#bib.bib25\" title=\"\">25</a>]</cite>. Similar models fine-tuned for Graphiti prompts may enhance knowledge extraction, particularly for complex conversations. Additionally, while current research on LLM-generated knowledge graphs has primarily operated without formal ontologies <cite class=\"ltx_cite ltx_citemacro_cite\">[<a class=\"ltx_ref\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/html/2501.13956v1#bib.bib9\" title=\"\">9</a>]</cite><cite class=\"ltx_cite ltx_citemacro_cite\">[<a class=\"ltx_ref\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/html/2501.13956v1#bib.bib4\" title=\"\">4</a>]</cite><cite class=\"ltx_cite ltx_citemacro_cite\">[<a class=\"ltx_ref\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/html/2501.13956v1#bib.bib17\" title=\"\">17</a>]</cite><cite class=\"ltx_cite ltx_citemacro_cite\">[<a class=\"ltx_ref\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/html/2501.13956v1#bib.bib19\" title=\"\">19</a>]</cite><cite class=\"ltx_cite ltx_citemacro_cite\">[<a class=\"ltx_ref\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/html/2501.13956v1#bib.bib26\" title=\"\">26</a>]</cite>, domain-specific ontologies present significant potential. Graph ontologies, foundational in pre-LLM knowledge graph work, warrant further exploration within the Graphiti framework.</p><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n</div>\n<div class=\"ltx_para ltx_noindent\" id=\"S5.p4\">\n<p class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S5.p4.1\">Our search for suitable memory benchmarks revealed limited options, with existing benchmarks often lacking robustness and complexity, frequently defaulting to simple needle-in-a-haystack fact-retrieval questions <cite class=\"ltx_cite ltx_citemacro_cite\">[<a class=\"ltx_ref\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/html/2501.13956v1#bib.bib3\" title=\"\">3</a>]</cite>. The field requires additional memory benchmarks, particularly those reflecting business applications like customer experience tasks, to effectively evaluate and differentiate memory approaches. Notably, no existing benchmarks adequately assess Zep’s capability to process and synthesize conversation history with structured business data. While Zep focuses on LLM memory, its traditional RAG capabilities should be evaluated against established benchmarks such as those in <cite class=\"ltx_cite ltx_citemacro_cite\">[<a class=\"ltx_ref\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/html/2501.13956v1#bib.bib17\" title=\"\">17</a>]</cite>, <cite class=\"ltx_cite ltx_citemacro_cite\">[<a class=\"ltx_ref\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/html/2501.13956v1#bib.bib27\" title=\"\">27</a>]</cite>, and <cite class=\"ltx_cite ltx_citemacro_cite\">[<a class=\"ltx_ref\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/html/2501.13956v1#bib.bib28\" title=\"\">28</a>]</cite>.</p><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n</div>\n<div class=\"ltx_para ltx_noindent\" id=\"S5.p5\">\n<p class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S5.p5.1\">Current literature on LLM memory and RAG systems insufficiently addresses production system scalability in terms of cost and latency. We have included latency benchmarks for our retrieval mechanisms to begin addressing this gap, following the example set by LightRAG’s authors in prioritizing these metrics.</p><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n</div>\n</section>\n<section class=\"ltx_section\" id=\"S6\">\n<h2 class=\"ltx_title ltx_title_section\">\n<span class=\"ltx_tag ltx_tag_section\">6 </span>Appendix</h2><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n<section class=\"ltx_subsection\" id=\"S6.SS1\">\n<h3 class=\"ltx_title ltx_title_subsection\">\n<span class=\"ltx_tag ltx_tag_subsection\">6.1 </span>Graph Construction Prompts</h3><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n<section class=\"ltx_subsubsection\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1\">\n<h4 class=\"ltx_title ltx_title_subsubsection\">\n<span class=\"ltx_tag ltx_tag_subsubsection\">6.1.1 </span>Entity Extraction</h4><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n<div class=\"ltx_para ltx_noindent\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1\">\n<p class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1\">\n<span class=\"ltx_inline-block ltx_parbox ltx_align_middle ltx_framed ltx_framed_rectangle\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1\" style=\"width:433.6pt;\">\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.1\">&lt;PREVIOUS MESSAGES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.2\">{previous_messages}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.3\">&lt;/PREVIOUS MESSAGES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.4\">&lt;CURRENT MESSAGE&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.5\">{current_message}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.6\">&lt;/CURRENT MESSAGE&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.7\">Given the above conversation, extract entity nodes from the\nCURRENT MESSAGE that are explicitly or implicitly mentioned:</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.8\">Guidelines:</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.9\">1. ALWAYS extract the speaker/actor as the first node. The speaker\nis the part before the colon in each line of dialogue.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.10\">2. Extract other significant entities, concepts, or actors mentioned\nin the CURRENT MESSAGE.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.11\">3. DO NOT create nodes for relationships or actions.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.12\">4. DO NOT create nodes for temporal information like dates, times\nor years (these will be added to edges later).</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.13\">5. Be as explicit as possible in your node names, using full names.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS1.p1.1.1.14\">6. DO NOT extract entities mentioned only</span>\n</span></p><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n</div>\n</section>\n<section class=\"ltx_subsubsection\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2\">\n<h4 class=\"ltx_title ltx_title_subsubsection\">\n<span class=\"ltx_tag ltx_tag_subsubsection\">6.1.2 </span>Entity Resolution</h4><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n<div class=\"ltx_para ltx_noindent\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1\">\n<p class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1\">\n<span class=\"ltx_inline-block ltx_parbox ltx_align_middle ltx_framed ltx_framed_rectangle\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1\" style=\"width:433.6pt;\">\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.1\">&lt;PREVIOUS MESSAGES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.2\">{previous_messages}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.3\">&lt;/PREVIOUS MESSAGES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.4\">&lt;CURRENT MESSAGE&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.5\">{current_message}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.6\">&lt;/CURRENT MESSAGE&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.7\">&lt;EXISTING NODES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.8\">{existing_nodes}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.9\">&lt;/EXISTING NODES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.10\">Given the above EXISTING NODES, MESSAGE, and PREVIOUS MESSAGES. Determine if the NEW NODE extracted from the conversation\nis a duplicate entity of one of the EXISTING NODES.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.11\">&lt;NEW NODE&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.12\">{new_node}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.13\">&lt;/NEW NODE&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.14\">Task:</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.15\">1. If the New Node represents the same entity as any node in Existing Nodes,\nreturn ’is_duplicate: true’ in the\nresponse. Otherwise, return ’is_duplicate: false’</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.16\">2. If is_duplicate is true, also return the uuid of the existing node in the\nresponse</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.17\">3. If is_duplicate is true, return a name for the node that is the most complete\nfull name.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.18\">Guidelines:</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS2.p1.1.1.19\">1. Use both the name and summary of nodes to determine if the entities are\nduplicates, duplicate nodes may have different names</span>\n</span></p><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n</div>\n</section>\n<section class=\"ltx_subsubsection\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3\">\n<h4 class=\"ltx_title ltx_title_subsubsection\">\n<span class=\"ltx_tag ltx_tag_subsubsection\">6.1.3 </span>Fact Extraction</h4><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n<div class=\"ltx_para ltx_noindent\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1\">\n<p class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1\"><span class=\"ltx_text ltx_framed ltx_framed_rectangle\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1\" style=\"border-color: #000000;\">\n<span class=\"ltx_inline-block ltx_parbox ltx_align_middle\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1\" style=\"width:433.6pt;\">\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.1\">&lt;PREVIOUS MESSAGES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.2\">{previous_messages}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.3\">&lt;/PREVIOUS MESSAGES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.4\">&lt;CURRENT MESSAGE&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.5\">{current_message}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.6\">&lt;/CURRENT MESSAGE&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.7\">&lt;ENTITIES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.8\">{entities}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.9\">&lt;/ENTITIES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.10\">Given the above MESSAGES and ENTITIES, extract all facts pertaining to the\nlisted ENTITIES from the CURRENT MESSAGE.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.11\">Guidelines:</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.12\">1. Extract facts only between the provided entities.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.13\">2. Each fact should represent a clear relationship between two DISTINCT nodes.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.14\">3. The relation_type should be a concise, all-caps description of the\nfact (e.g., LOVES, IS_FRIENDS_WITH, WORKS_FOR).</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.15\">4. Provide a more detailed fact containing all relevant information.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS3.p1.1.1.1.16\">5. Consider temporal aspects of relationships when relevant.</span>\n</span></span></p><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n</div>\n</section>\n<section class=\"ltx_subsubsection\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4\">\n<h4 class=\"ltx_title ltx_title_subsubsection\">\n<span class=\"ltx_tag ltx_tag_subsubsection\">6.1.4 </span>Fact Resolution</h4><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n<div class=\"ltx_para ltx_noindent\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1\">\n<p class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1\">\n<span class=\"ltx_inline-block ltx_parbox ltx_align_middle ltx_framed ltx_framed_rectangle\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1\" style=\"width:433.6pt;\">\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1.1\">Given the following context, determine whether the New Edge represents any\nof the edges in the list of Existing Edges.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1.2\">&lt;EXISTING EDGES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1.3\">{existing_edges}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1.4\">&lt;/EXISTING EDGES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1.5\">&lt;NEW EDGE&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1.6\">{new_edge}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1.7\">&lt;/NEW EDGE&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1.8\">Task:</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1.9\">1. If the New Edges represents the same factual information as any edge\nin Existing Edges, return ’is_duplicate: true’ in the response.\nOtherwise, return ’is_duplicate: false’</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1.10\">2. If is_duplicate is true, also return the uuid of the existing edge in\nthe response</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1.11\">Guidelines:</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS4.p1.1.1.12\">1. The facts do not need to be completely identical to be duplicates,\nthey just need to express the same information.</span>\n</span></p><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n</div>\n</section>\n<section class=\"ltx_subsubsection\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5\">\n<h4 class=\"ltx_title ltx_title_subsubsection\">\n<span class=\"ltx_tag ltx_tag_subsubsection\">6.1.5 </span>Temporal Extraction</h4><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n<div class=\"ltx_para ltx_noindent\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1\">\n<p class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1\">\n<span class=\"ltx_inline-block ltx_parbox ltx_align_middle ltx_framed ltx_framed_rectangle\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1\" style=\"width:433.6pt;\">\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.1\">&lt;PREVIOUS MESSAGES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.2\">{previous_messages}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.3\">&lt;/PREVIOUS MESSAGES&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.4\">&lt;CURRENT MESSAGE&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.5\">{current_message}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.6\">&lt;/CURRENT MESSAGE&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.7\">&lt;REFERENCE TIMESTAMP&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.8\">{reference_timestamp}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.9\">&lt;/REFERENCE TIMESTAMP&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.10\">&lt;FACT&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.11\">{fact}</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.12\">&lt;/FACT&gt;</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.13\">IMPORTANT: Only extract time information if it is part of the provided fact.\nOtherwise ignore the time mentioned.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.14\">Make sure to do your best to determine the dates if only the relative time is\nmentioned. (eg 10 years ago, 2 mins ago) based on the provided reference\ntimestamp</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.15\">If the relationship is not of spanning nature, but you are still able to\ndetermine the dates, set the valid_at only.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.16\">Definitions:</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.17\">- valid_at: The date and time when the relationship described by the\nedge fact became true or was established.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.18\">- invalid_at: The date and time when the relationship described by the\nedge fact stopped being true or ended.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.19\">Task:</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.20\">Analyze the conversation and determine if there are dates that are part\nof the edge fact. Only set dates if they explicitly relate to the\nformation or alteration of the relationship itself.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.21\">Guidelines:</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.22\">1. Use ISO 8601 format (YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS.SSSSSSZ) for datetimes.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.23\">2. Use the reference timestamp as the current time when determining\nthe valid_at and invalid_at dates.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.24\">3. If the fact is written in the present tense, use the Reference\nTimestamp for the valid_at date</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.25\">4. If no temporal information is found that establishes or changes the\nrelationship, leave the fields as null.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.26\">5. Do not infer dates from related events. Only use dates that are\ndirectly stated to establish or change the relationship.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.27\">6. For relative time mentions directly related to the relationship, calculate the\nactual datetime based on the reference timestamp.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.28\">7. If only a date is mentioned without a specific time, use 00:00:00 (midnight) for that date.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.29\">8. If only year is mentioned, use January 1st of that year at 00:00:00.</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_p\" id=\"S6.SS1.SSS5.p1.1.1.30\">9. Always include the time zone offset (use Z for UTC if no specific\ntime zone is mentioned).</span>\n</span></p><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n</div>\n</section>\n</section>\n</section>\n<section class=\"ltx_bibliography\" id=\"bib\">\n<h2 class=\"ltx_title ltx_title_bibliography\">References</h2><button class=\"sr-only button\" style=\"display: none;\">Report issue for preceding element</button>\n<ul class=\"ltx_biblist\">\n<li class=\"ltx_bibitem\" id=\"bib.bib1\">\n<span class=\"ltx_tag ltx_tag_bibitem\">[1]</span>\n<span class=\"ltx_bibblock\">\nAshish Vaswani, Noam Shazeer, Niki Parmar, Jakob Uszkoreit, Llion Jones, Aidan&nbsp;N.
